In this context, we present HexMIL (Hierarchical EXplainable Multiple Instance Learning), a mask-free medical deepfake detector that simultaneously addresses both limitations using only binary volume-level supervision. HexMIL decomposes each CT volume into a two-level hierarchy of patches and slices, aggregated via independent Gated Attention modules whose weights are directly combined into a full-resolution 3D attention volume that localizes the manipulated sub-region without any pixel-level annotation. Unlike post-hoc methods such as Grad-CAM, HexMIL's attention weights constitute the exact forward computation driving the classification decision, providing ante-hoc and structurally faithful spatial attribution. We evaluate HexMIL on M3DSynth and CT-GAN datasets under a rigorous cross-generator generalization protocol, training on a single generative architecture and testing on unseen ones. HexMIL outperforms all baselines by $+9.1$ AUC and $+9.4$ F1 in out-of-domain classification, and achieves the best average IoU and Pointing Game score in localization. Project page: opontorno.github.io/hexmil.

</details>


### [105] [Robust and Efficient Motion Reasoning for Privacy-Aware Classroom Incident Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05115)
*Paritosh Parmar,Landy Lan,Hong Yang,Chen Yi,Chiat Pin Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向教室安全监控的隐私感知、计算高效的课堂事件识别框架，通过分层运动学表征和知识蒸馏，在极低计算成本下超越更大规模的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教室安全事故识别在真实部署中面临隐私、计算效率和泛化三大挑战，但该领域缺乏基准和专门方法。现有方法多依赖姿态或外观特征，难以在CCTV风格的低质量监控画面中有效区分事件。

Method: 1) 提出混合基准：结合生成式CCTV风格视频和真实教室姿态数据；2) 构建分层运动学表征（方向、速度、加速度、强度等多阶运动特征）；3) 通过知识蒸馏将大型教师模型的多阶分层运动推理能力压缩到小型单阶学生模型，实现高效逐人推理。

Result: 模型在不到基线十分之一的计算成本下超越了更大的基线模型，展现出更强的域外运动推理能力和零样本合成到真实的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 运动学推理结合知识蒸馏能够实现隐私感知且高效的教室安全事件识别，作者将公开基准、代码和工具以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Can computer vision help make classrooms safer? In this pilot study, we investigate privacy-aware and computationally efficient classroom incident recognition from CCTV-style observations. This setting remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and few methods designed for the privacy, efficiency, and generalization demands of real-world deployment. We introduce a novel hybrid benchmark combining generative CCTV-style videos with real-world classroom pose data, and propose a lightweight, but robust motion-reasoning framework motivated by the observation that many incidents differ more in motion direction, speed, acceleration, and intensity than in pose alone. To that end, our method first constructs hierarchical kinematic representations of human actions. Our method then distills hierarchical, multi-order kinematic reasoning from a large teacher into a much smaller single-order student, enabling efficient per-person inference while preserving expressive motion understanding. Experiments show that our model outperforms substantially larger baselines at less than one-tenth of their computational cost, while also demonstrating stronger out-of-domain motion reasoning and zero-shot synthetic-to-real generalization. We will publicly release the benchmark, codebase, and supporting tools to facilitate further research in privacy-aware classroom safety.

</details>


### [106] [IRIS: A Visual Cortex-Inspired Framework for Analyzing Orientation Selectivity in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05122)
*Vaishnavi B Mohan,Vijayakrishna Naganoor,Yashas Annadani,Shashank Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过神经科学启发的度量方法（RSS、ORS、方向调谐带宽）系统研究了视觉Transformer（ViT）中方向选择性的涌现机制，发现训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，并提供了如何根据这些度量决定解冻层数以优化下游泛化的启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT已成为视觉编码的标准架构，但由于缺乏归纳偏置，其如何编码低级特征（如方向选择性）在机制上仍不清晰。借鉴生物视觉系统中初级视觉皮层方向选择性的形成机制，作者希望探究这些生物学基础特征是否在ViT中涌现。

Method: 引入三个神经科学启发的度量：表征相似性得分（RSS）、方向招募得分（ORS）和方向调谐带宽，用于量化方向在表征几何中的编码方式及其随模型深度的变化。通过在多种训练范式和模型规模下进行系统分析，追踪方向选择性的涌现过程。

Result: （1）训练范式是方向选择性的最强决定因素，共享目标函数的模型在相对深度上峰值一致，与规模无关；（2）训练早期许多单元已具方向选择性，早至中层随时间招募更多此类单元，而深层单元逐渐失去选择性并转向语义编码；（3）所提出的度量可作为决定解冻层数以获得最佳下游泛化效果的机制启发式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种在ViT训练过程中追踪生物学基础特征的方法，揭示了ViT如何编码所需属性，并系统地建立了跨任务泛化机制的理解。

Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) have become the de facto standard for image encoding across many perception tasks. Despite their empirical success, it remains mechanistically unclear how they encode low-level features, given their lack of inductive biases: ViTs process information globally rather than relying on local structure. Biological visual systems, in contrast, build low-level features, such as orientation selectivity in the primary visual cortex, by combining information from small, localized regions of the visual field. These features are general-purpose representations, shared and required across multiple specialized neural pathways, unlike higher-level, task-specific semantic features. This raises the question if such biologically-grounded features arise in ViTs. In this work, we systematically study how orientation selectivity emerges in ViTs by introducing a suite of neuroscience-inspired metrics: representational similarity score (RSS), orientation recruitment score (ORS), and orientation tuning bandwidth to quantify how orientation is encoded in representational geometry and as a function of model depth. Through extensive analysis, we find that: (1) the training paradigm is the strongest determinant of orientation selectivity, with models sharing an objective, peaking at comparable relative depths regardless of scale (2) many units are orientation-selective early in training, with early-to-middle layers recruiting more such units over time, while deeper layers lose selectivity and broaden their tuning toward semantic encoding and (3) our metrics offer a mechanistic heuristic for how many layers to unfreeze for best downstream generalization. Our framework presents a way to track biologically-grounded features during ViT training, probes how desired properties are encoded in transformer representations, and builds a systematic understanding of how ViTs generalize across tasks.

</details>


### [107] [OPD-V: Visual On-Policy Self-Distillation with Modality Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05131)
*Aniri,Jinhe Bi,Peng Liao,Zengjie Jin,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OPD-V，一种基于模态平衡的视觉OPSD（同策略自蒸馏）范式，通过正负教师构建"模态平衡信任区域"来选择自蒸馏的token，从而改善多模态大语言模型中的"模态失衡"问题，在6个基准、4种骨干和5种后训练方法上均提升推理性能并降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD方法利用多源输入的"特权信息"引导自蒸馏，但忽视了MLLM推理中固有的"模态失衡"挑战——当文本信息主导生成时，模型无法充分整合多模态输入，导致精心设计的特权信息未被充分利用，限制了OPSD的有效性。

Method: 通过构造"正教师"（使用放大图像）和"负教师"（使用掩码图像）来产生不同程度的模态失衡，发现"模态平衡"本身可作为特权信息。进而提出OPD-V范式：利用正负教师的模态平衡对数边际差异定义"模态平衡信任区域"，从中选择用于自蒸馏的同策略token。

Result: 在6个基准测试、4种MLLM骨干网络和5种后训练方法上进行实验，OPD-V一致性地提升了推理性能，同时降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: 模态平衡本身可以作为有效的特权信息用于OPSD，OPD-V通过信任区域选择策略实现了更高效、更低成本的多模态推理优化，弥补了现有方法忽视模态失衡的不足。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has become a standard post-training approach for improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing methods draw privileged information from diverse input sources to guide self-distillation. Yet these designs overlook Modality Imbalance, a challenge inherent to MLLM reasoning. When textual information dominates generation, the model cannot fully integrate its multimodal input. Consequently, carefully designed privileged information remains underused, limiting the effectiveness of OPSD. To examine this limitation, we construct a Positive Teacher with the Zoom-In Image and a Negative Teacher with the Mask Image, which exhibit different degrees of Modality Imbalance. Changes in their reasoning correctness and token logits reveal that Modality Balance can itself serve as privileged information. Motivated by this finding, we introduce OPD-V, a visual OPSD paradigm that instantiates such information through the Positive Teacher and Negative Teacher. Positive Modality-Balance Logits Margins define a Modality-Balance Trust Region that selects the on-policy tokens used for self-distillation. Experiments across 6 benchmarks, 4 MLLM backbones, and 5 post-training methods show that OPD-V consistently improves reasoning performance while reducing training cost.

</details>


### [108] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN模型，通过预测子皮层结构表面的第一基本形式（度量张量）在连续时间内的演变，并使用可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible求解器将预测的度量解码为有效表面，实现对纵向网格形状变化的预测，在ADNI数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测皮层下结构形状随时间的演变对于预后判断和临床试验入组具有重要意义，但现有纵向网格预测方法要么通过高维嵌入外推形状轨迹，要么直接回归顶点变形，缺乏有效利用表面内在几何信息的方法。

Method: 提出MT-GNN模型：单个逐结构的图网络，以傅里叶编码的领先时间（lead time）为条件，对任意因果多次就诊历史和任意预测时域，预测每个顶点第一基本形式（度量张量）；通过可微的As-Rigid-As-Possible求解器将预测度量解码为表面，并以刚体对齐后的顶点误差进行端到端训练。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的14个子皮层结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时域上表现最佳（平均顶点误差较时间均值降低2.29%，p=6.1×10⁻⁵，在14/14个结构上胜出），优于测地形状回归（DCM，-0.19%）和网格Transformer（TransforMesh，-0.45%；p=1.2×10⁻⁴），且优势随预测时域增长而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过预测表面内在几何（度量张量）而非直接回归顶点变形，结合可微表面重建的端到端训练，可以产生更精确且始终有效的表面预测，且优势在长时域预测中更加明显。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [109] [SmartMage: Dynamic Modality Orchestration for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05137)
*Yue Zhang,Yingzhao Jian,Yunqiu Xu,Xiaoxiao Sun,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartMage，一个统一的多模态大语言模型，通过动态编排异构模态（视觉和几何）实现语义感知的3D场景理解，解决了现有MLLM固定模态组合忽视查询依赖性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM依赖固定的模态组合，忽视了不同查询对模态的不同需求，导致从无关模态引入语义噪声，同时未充分利用信息量更大的模态，造成计算浪费和推理能力稀释。

Method: SmartMage包含两个核心模块：(1) Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART)模块，利用语义先验、文本-模态对齐和模态质量来选择任务相关模态；(2) Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE)模块，利用模态先验指导专家激活，促进多模态推理中的自适应专业化。

Result: SmartMage在5个3D场景理解基准上达到最先进性能，在RGB-only视频理解基准上取得有竞争力的结果。在诊断基准ScanFacet上的细粒度语义分类分析中，观察到模态-语义模式，进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态按语义需求自适应选择模态组合能够有效提升3D场景理解性能，避免固定模态组合带来的噪声和计算浪费，为模态路由和专家激活提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes is fundamental to embodied intelligence, requiring joint reasoning over heterogeneous information from multiple modalities, including visual and geometric cues. However, the relevance of these modalities often varies across queries. Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically rely on fixed modality combinations, overlooking query-dependent modality needs. Such a rigid design can introduce semantic noise from irrelevant modalities while underutilizing more informative ones, leading to wasted computation and diluted reasoning. To address these challenges, this paper proposes SmartMage, a unified MLLM that dynamically orchestrates heterogeneous modalities for semantic-aware 3D scene understanding. Specifically, SmartMage incorporates: (1) a Semantic-guided Modality Adaptive RouTng (SMART) module that selects task-relevant modalities using semantic priors, text-modality alignment, and modality quality; and (2) a Modality-Aware Gating Expert (MAGE) module that leverages modality priors to guide expert activation, fostering adaptive specialization in multimodal reasoning. Empirically, SmartMage achieves state-of-the-art performance across five 3D scene understanding benchmarks, and attains competitive results on RGB-only video understanding benchmarks. In our diagnostic benchmark ScanFacet, tasks are divided into fine-grained semantic categories, enabling analysis of modality combinations preferred by each semantic type. The observed modality-semantic patterns provide further evidence of SmartMage's effectiveness. Project page: https://yuecheong.github.io/SmartMage/.

</details>


### [110] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AV-MSF，一种从多视角图像和少量撞击声录音中重建物体级声场的表示方法，结合3D高斯泼溅与物理模态参数，实现少样本冲击声渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建仅聚焦几何和外观，忽略物体撞击声所蕴含的材料、刚度等物理属性；现有冲击声建模要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大量数据，缺乏高效且物理可解释的方案。

Method: AV-MSF基于3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）集成密集3D视觉特征以提供几何感知先验，使用紧凑且物理有意义的模态参数表示冲击声场，实现少样本鲁棒重建。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上，AV-MSF在冲击声渲染任务上达到最先进水平，优于基于物理仿真和数据驱动的基线方法，并支持接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。

Conclusion: AV-MSF证明了将视觉几何先验与物理模态声学表示结合的有效性，为物体级声学重建提供了新思路，并展示了广泛的下游应用潜力。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [111] [CoCo-IR: Contextual Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05149)
*Shengcao Cao,Tanmaya Shekhar Dabral,Zhongli Ding,Madhuri Shanbhogue,Kaifeng Chen,Zhe Li,Mojtaba Seyedhosseini,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoCo-IR（上下文组合图像检索）这一新任务，使检索系统支持多轮交互式图像搜索。作者基于大型多模态模型（LMM）构建了一个上下文感知推理器，生成可跨轮次演化的可变换图像嵌入（TIE），并设计了全自动数据引擎来生成训练数据。实验在CIRCO单轮基准上达到39.4 mAP@5，在4轮对话的CoCo-IR基准上达到44.1 R@1，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于指令的图像检索系统仅支持单轮交互，无法捕捉复杂现实视觉搜索中迭代式地逐步细化的需求过程。

Method: 提出基于大型多模态模型（LMM）的上下文感知推理器，利用完整交互历史生成可跨轮次演化的变换图像嵌入（TIE）；同时开发全自动、可扩展的数据引擎，利用LMM生成高质量上下文检索数据，并通过模型引导验证挖掘困难负样本。

Result: 在CIRCO单轮基准上达到39.4 mAP@5；在CoCo-IR基准的4轮对话中取得44.1 R@1，远超现有方法（28.2 R@1），建立了新的最优性能。

Conclusion: CoCo-IR方法成功解决了多轮交互式图像检索问题，证明了利用LMM作为上下文感知推理器能够有效处理迭代式视觉搜索任务，且数据引擎无需人工标注即可规模化生成高质量训练数据。

Abstract: Current instruction-based image retrieval systems are powerful but limited to single-turn interactions, failing to capture the iterative nature of complex, real-world visual searches. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contextual Composed Image Retrieval (CoCo-IR), a novel task that enables users to progressively refine search results through interactions. We address this new task by proposing a new model based on a Large Multimodal Model (LMM) that functions as a context-aware reasoner for CoCo-IR. Our model interprets the entire interaction history to generate Transformable Image Embeddings (TIE) that evolve across turns. To fuel the model training without expensive human annotations, we develop a fully autonomous, scalable data engine that leverages LMMs to generate high-quality contextual retrieval data, and uses model-guided verification to mine challenging hard negatives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach establishes new state-of-the-art performance: We achieve 39.4 mAP@5 on the challenging single-turn benchmark CIRCO; furthermore, on our new CoCo-IR benchmark, our model maintains robust performance with 44.1 R@1 on 4-turn dialogues, dramatically outperforming existing methods (28.2 4-turn R@1) that fail to handle multi-turn context. Project page: https://CoCo-IR.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Transfer Learning for Named Entity Recognition of Classical Latin through LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04015)
*Callum Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文描述了渥太华大学团队参与EvaLatin 2026共享任务，利用商业LLM（gemini-2.5-pro和claude-sonnet-4-5）通过提示工程完成古典拉丁语命名实体识别（NER）任务，在粗粒度（11类）和细粒度（28类）两个子任务中均取得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典拉丁语属于资源匮乏的低资源语言，传统NLP方法在该领域表现有限。作者旨在利用现代LLM的跨语言迁移学习能力，验证商业大模型在古典语言处理任务上的潜力。

Method: 使用商业LLM（gemini-2.5-pro和claude-sonnet-4-5），通过提示工程（prompt engineering）方法完成NER任务。任务分为粗粒度（11类）和细粒度（28类）两个子任务，每种场景在严格（strict）和模糊（fuzzy）两种评估体制下进行评测。

Result: 在所有子任务和所有评估指标/体制中均排名第一，获得了最佳成绩。

Conclusion: 商业LLM通过跨语言迁移学习可以有效地服务于古典拉丁语这类低资源语言的NER任务，弥补了该领域数据不足和传统方法局限的短板。

Abstract: With the increase in digitized resources of Classical Latin texts and modern breakthroughs of Large Language Models (LLMs), I contribute to ancient language research by participating in EvaLatin 2026. This paper describes Team uOttawa's system description and results for the Named Entity Recognition (NER) shared task. The task is divided into two subtasks: coarse-grained NER with 11 classes and fine-grained NER with 28 classes, each evaluated under strict and fuzzy regimes. Through prompt engineering of commercial LLMs gemini-2.5-pro and claude-sonnet-4-5, I show that the underrepresented ancient Latin language can take advantage of cross-lingual transfer learning by using advancements made by the wider LLM development community. Overall, the methods discussed in this report demonstrate very strong results, placing first in both NER subtasks and achieving the best scores across all evaluation metrics and regimes among all submissions.

</details>


### [113] [When More Becomes Less: Position-Dependent Repetition Effects in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04021)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现基于完形填空的重复目标探测实验存在位置效应：当读槽紧邻重复块时，目标概率随重复次数N单调上升并趋于平台；但当读槽被放置在全新句子框架中时，目标概率呈现倒U型曲线（先升后降）。该效应在13个开源编码器和解码器模型及42种多语言组合中一致复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的完形填空式探测实验在变化目标词重复次数时，隐含假设"更多副本对预测的影响与读槽位置无关"。作者质疑这一假设，认为读槽位置可能与重复计数效应存在交互作用。

Method: 设计了双探针实验：相邻探针（读槽紧邻重复块）和位移探针（读槽位于新句子框架内）。通过六条件因果消融排除长度、冗余和语义邻居等混淆因素；通过框架语用学对照排除读槽框架的伪效应；并分析了注意力分配内部机制。

Result: 相邻重复符合启动直觉（N增大概率上升并平台化）；位移重复产生倒U型曲线（先升后降）。每词下降的bootstrap置信区间在13个模型中均排除零，42/42多语言组合中复现。内部机制显示：因果LM中每目标token注意力随N下降但重复块总预算增长，而掩码LM中无此现象。

Conclusion: 探测实验变化重复次数时，不能将读槽位置视为与测量内容正交的变量。重复计数效应依赖于读槽位置，这对完形填空式探测实验的设计和解释提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Cloze-style probes that vary how often a target token appears implicitly assume that more copies of a target affect prediction the same way regardless of where the readout slot sits. We show this assumption fails. Our two-probe design holds a repeated-target prefix fixed and varies only the readout position: the adjacent probe places the slot immediately after the repeated block; the displaced probe places it inside a fresh sentence frame. Adjacent repetition behaves as priming intuition predicts: $P(\text{target})$ climbs with $N$ and plateaus. Displaced repetition produces an inverted-U: $P(\text{target})$ rises to an early peak and then declines as more copies are added. The displaced inverted-U shows a per-word drop with bootstrap CI excluding zero in all 13 open-access encoder and decoder models we test, and replicates across Spanish, Chinese, German, and French in 42 of 42 multilingual cells. A six-condition causal ablation isolates the effect to exact lexical repetition rather than length, generic redundancy, or semantic-neighbour exposure. A frame-pragmatics control rules out an artefact of the readout frame. Internally, per-target-token attention falls with $N$ while the total budget assigned to the repeated block grows in causal LMs but not in the masked LM we probe. Probes that vary repetition count cannot treat the readout position as orthogonal to what they measure.

</details>


### [114] [MIDAS: Multi-LLM Iterative Data-Adaptive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04307)
*Karen Lee,Dhanashree Balaram,Seojun Shon,Umair Rasheed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MIDAS，一个多LLM迭代数据自适应摘要框架，通过数据驱动的模式学习和用例特定的个性化，实现自动适配不同摘要需求，在客服工单摘要任务上相比SOTA方法在ROUGE和BERTScore指标上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实企业的摘要任务（如支持工单、法律文档、事故报告）需要严格遵循领域特定的指南、输出格式和组织惯例，而手动编写和维护提示词成本高昂。现有基于LLM批评驱动的自动提示优化方法受限于静态提示，无法适应摘要应用的多样性。

Method: 提出MIDAS框架，采用多LLM协作，结合数据驱动的模式学习和用例特定个性化，扩展了LLM批评驱动的提示优化范式，无需人工提示工程即可自动适应不同摘要需求。在五个输出格式的企业客服工单摘要任务上进行验证，并测试了跨模型和跨领域泛化能力。

Result: 在五个输出格式的客服工单摘要任务中，MIDAS相比CriSPO和ZERA等SOTA方法，ROUGE-1最高提升11.0%，ROUGE-2最高提升18.2%，ROUGE-L最高提升8.0%，并在所有格式和输出类型上一致提升BERTScore F1。同时通过多LLM配置和金融领域基准展示了跨模型与跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MIDAS通过多LLM协作和数据自适应学习，有效解决了企业文本摘要中静态提示无法适应多样化需求的问题，在摘要质量和泛化性方面均优于现有方法，展示了无需人工提示工程即可自动适配不同摘要场景的潜力。

Abstract: Text summarization is deceptively difficult. While condensing information seems straightforward, real-world enterprise summarization of support tickets, legal documents, incident reports, and more, demands strict adherence to domain-specific guidelines, output formats, and organizational conventions. Crafting prompts that reliably satisfy these constraints is labor-intensive, requiring significant human expertise and continuous maintenance as requirements evolve. Existing automated prompt optimization methods reduce this burden through Large Language Model (LLM) critique-driven refinement, yet remain limited by static prompts that cannot adapt to the diversity of summary applications. We propose Multi-LLM Iterative Data-Adaptive Summarization (MIDAS), a multi-LLM framework that extends this paradigm with data-driven pattern learning and use-case-specific personalization, enabling automatic adaptation to different summarization requirements without manual prompt engineering. Applied to enterprise customer ticket summarization across five output formats, MIDAS achieves the strongest overall performance against state-of-the-art critique-driven optimization frameworks such as CriSPO and ZERA, improving ROUGE-1 by up to 11.0%, ROUGE-2 by up to 18.2%, and ROUGE-L by up to 8.0%, while consistently improving BERTScore F1 across all formats and output types. We additionally demonstrate cross-model and cross-domain generalization through multi-LLM configurations and finance-domain summarization benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [Reconstructing Persistent Worlds from Narratives for Narrative-Grounded Interactive Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04037)
*Yi-Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将"从叙事描述中重建显式持久世界"作为叙事驱动交互体验的核心计算目标，而非将世界视为下游生成的隐式副产品。作者开发了一个参考原型，从叙事中重建结构化持久世界表征并实例化可玩的基于网格的环境，通过三个案例研究证明了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计叙事驱动的交互式体验仍然劳动密集，因为交互内容必须与叙事隐含的底层世界保持一致。现有方法分别处理叙事规划、场景生成和游戏玩法生成，各自构建针对特定下游任务的计算表征，而非显式重建和维护支撑它们的持久世界。

Method: 提出将显式持久世界重建作为核心计算目标，从叙事描述中重建并维护持久实体、位置、语义关系和演化中的世界状态，仅推断支持连贯交互体验所需的上下文信息。开发了一个参考原型，重建结构化持久世界表征，并实例化可玩的基于网格的环境。

Result: 通过三个代表性案例研究（程序化场景、原创奇幻叙事、改编公共领域故事），证明了重建持久世界的可行性，并展示共享世界表征如何在保持源叙事的基础上支持连贯的游戏玩法。

Conclusion: 通过在交互实现之前显式重建持久世界，这项工作桥接了计算叙事理解与交互内容生成，为AI辅助游戏创作、混合主动性设计、教育模拟和叙事驱动的交互体验提供了语义基础。

Abstract: Designing narrative-grounded interactive experiences remains labor-intensive because interactive content must align with the underlying world implied by the narrative. Existing approaches formulate problems such as narrative planning, scene generation, and gameplay generation, each constructing computational representations tailored to specific downstream tasks rather than explicitly reconstructing and maintaining the persistent world that grounds them.
  We investigate reconstructing explicit persistent worlds from narrative descriptions as the central computational objective for narrative-grounded interactive realization. Rather than treating the world as an implicit by-product of downstream generation, our approach reconstructs and maintains persistent entities, locations, semantic relationships, and evolving world states while inferring only the contextual information required to support coherent interactive experiences.
  To investigate this perspective, we develop a reference prototype that reconstructs structured persistent world representations from narrative descriptions and subsequently instantiates playable tile-based environments. Through three representative case studies spanning a procedural scenario, an original fantasy narrative, and an adapted public-domain story, we demonstrate the feasibility of reconstructing persistent worlds and show how a shared world representation supports coherent gameplay while remaining grounded in the source narrative.
  By explicitly reconstructing persistent worlds prior to interactive realization, this work bridges computational narrative understanding and interactive content generation, providing a semantic foundation for AI-assisted game authoring, mixed-initiative design, educational simulations, and narrative-grounded interactive experiences.

</details>


### [116] [OneDayAgent: Towards a Long-Horizon Harness for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05013)
*Jingsheng Zheng,Xinyuan Fang,Jintian Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OneDayAgent是一个面向长期跨环境多模态任务的智能体框架，通过将开放式请求分解为有界子任务、维护执行记忆并在上下文压力下进行校验和修复，在AgentIF-OneDay基准上取得SOTA成绩（0.821），且无需调参即可泛化到五个不同后端LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体研究虽然分别解决了目标漂移、状态丢失和上下文溢出等单一失败模式，但缺乏一个统一的框架能够同时管理这些问题，并且在不同的后端模型上保持有效性。

Method: OneDayAgent将开放式请求转化为受管理的执行过程：将任务分解为有边界的子任务，在上下文压力下维护执行记忆，并对最终交付物进行验证和修复。

Result: 在AgentIF-OneDay的104个任务上，使用GLM-5.2后端时整体得分0.821，达到新的SOTA。同一框架在来自三个模型家族的五个后端LLM上无需调参即可运行，表明该框架具有良好的后端泛化能力。

Conclusion: OneDayAgent证明了单一的统一框架能够同时处理目标保持、状态管理和上下文控制等问题，并且可以跨不同后端模型有效泛化，尽管不同模型在同一工作流下会表现出不同的执行风格。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to open-ended everyday requests that span work, study, and life. These tasks are long-horizon, cross-environment, and multimodal, forcing the agent to preserve goals and constraints across many steps while navigating heterogeneous tools and attachments. While prior work has addressed individual failure modes such as goals drift, states loss, and context overflow, whether a single harness can manage them jointly and remain effective across backends has received less study. We present OneDayAgent, a long-horizon harness for autonomous agents. OneDayAgent turns an open-ended request into a managed execution process that decomposes tasks into bounded subtasks, maintains execution memory under context pressure, and verifies and repairs the final deliverable. We evaluate OneDayAgent on AgentIF-OneDay across 104 tasks. With the GLM-5.2 backend, OneDayAgent sets a new state of the art with an overall score of 0.821. The same harness runs across five backend LLMs from three model families, indicating the harness generalizes across backends without tuning, even as different models induce distinct execution styles under the same workflow.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Sexism Detection Using Multi-Agent Perspectivist Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04056)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Tina Shahedi,Robert A. Bagheri,Mehdi Dastani,Masoume M. Raeissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-PO框架，通过按标注行为而非人口属性对标注者聚类，并为每个聚类微调一个LLM智能体，结合个体与团队级奖励的偏好优化，来保留标注者在性别歧视文本标注中的真实分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们在标注性别歧视文本时存在真实且合理的观点分歧，而大多数NLP系统通过多数投票丢弃了这些分歧，忽略了不同视角的价值。

Method: 在EXIST 2024数据集（英语和西班牙语推文）上，首先按标注行为对标注者聚类，为每个聚类微调一个LLM智能体以复现该聚类的标注行为，并通过结合个体和团队级奖励的偏好优化来协调各智能体。

Result: 在四种设置（两种语言×两种骨干语言模型）中均发现：1）未经微调的智能体行为几乎相同，说明聚类特定训练是必要的；2）仅用本聚类标签训练会使智能体过度偏离其应代表的聚类，而加入共享的团队级训练信号能使每个智能体持续保持与聚类的校准。

Conclusion: MAP-PO框架通过多个智能体分别代表不同标注者视角，有效保留了标注分歧信息，团队级训练信号是确保各智能体准确代表所属聚类的关键。

Abstract: When people label text for sexism, they often disagree, and not because some of them are wrong: they genuinely perceive sexism differently. Most NLP systems discard this disagreement by collapsing it into a majority vote. We propose the Multi-Agent Perspectivist Preference Optimization (MAP-PO) framework to keep these different perspectives. On the EXIST 2024 dataset of labeled English and Spanish tweets, we first cluster annotators by their labeling behavior rather than their demographic attributes. We then fine-tune one Large Language Model agent per cluster to reproduce that cluster's annotation behavior, and coordinate the agents with preference optimization that combines individual and team-level rewards. We evaluate MAP-PO in four settings defined by two languages and two backbone language models, asking whether each agent reproduces the annotations of its own cluster and whether the agents together reproduce the majority label. Two findings hold in all four settings. First, without fine-tuning the agents behave almost identically, so cluster-specific training is necessary. Second, we show that training each agent only on the labels of its own cluster pushes the agents far beyond the clusters they should represent, while adding a shared team-level training signal consistently keeps each agent calibrated to its cluster.

</details>


### [118] [Mind the Cap: Output-Budget Regimes Change the Measured Multilingual Reasoning Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04160)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现多语言评估中的输出token上限（cap）是一个隐藏的实验变量：不同语言表达相同内容所需token数不同，导致单一token上限下的准确率比较存在偏差。通过系统扫描token预算，作者证明母语vs翻译的MGSM差距会随预算摆动高达57个点，并通过冻结测试、时序恒等式等方法验证了该效应，最终建议将输出上限作为独立变量，在完整预算范围内报告准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评估通常在单一输出token上限下报告准确率，但不同语言表达同一内容所需token数不同，因此token上限实际上是一个未被控制的隐藏变量，可能导致"母语vs翻译"的差距是token预算人为造成的假象，而非真实推理能力差异。

Method: 使用Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在MGSM（德语、泰语、斯瓦希里语）上，对四种提示策略系统扫描token预算范围；采用冻结的峰值测试（540,000次独立硬上限解码）、长度归一化、交叉拟合的泰语词汇扩展、以及正确发射时序恒等式来验证token预算效应。

Result: 母语vs翻译差距在不同budget下摆动高达57个点；长度归一化在预算受限时移动最多38.9个点；冻结的Holm校正测试拒绝所有零假设；在饱和点以上残余差异是策略性能差距而非推理缺陷；公告预算（而非仅实际强制预算）也会影响准确率（5.1个点差异）；时序恒等式精确预测峰值（误差0.65-0.92点）。

Conclusion: 输出token上限应被视为独立变量，准确率应在整个预算区间内报告，而非单一预算点。忽略token预算效应会误导多语言模型的能力评估结论。

Abstract: Multilingual evaluations report accuracy at a single output-token cap, but languages need different numbers of tokens to express the same content, so the cap is a hidden experimental variable. We test whether the native-vs-translate gap on MGSM (German, Thai, Swahili) is a token-budget artifact for Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct under four prompting strategies. The measured gap swings by up to 57 points across budgets, length normalization moves it by up to 38.9 points where the cap binds, and at tight caps normalization can reverse which strategy scores higher. We prospectively froze the sweep's three Qwen peaks and its near-zero value at 1024 and evaluated them on 540,000 independently hard-capped decodes: a second frozen family of six Holm-corrected tests rejects every null. The frozen test at $B^*=1024$ still fails to reject because native accuracy has already saturated there; above saturation, the residual difference is a strategy-performance gap, not an identified reasoning deficit. The same truncation channel prices a cost-ordered adaptation ladder: a cross-fitted Thai vocabulary extension closes 0.0 points of the gap at the frozen budget and 4.9 points where 19% of traces still truncate. A third frozen family varies only the announced budget at a fixed enforced cap; announcing 128 rather than 2048 tokens moves Thai native accuracy by 5.1 points, so accuracy is not a function of the enforced cap alone. A correct-emission timing identity computed from one long-cap run matches the three pre-specified MGSM peaks to 0.65 points and, in an exploratory Qwen-only analysis of three further benchmarks, tracks held-out items to 0.92 points, locating the peak exactly in five of seven cells. Treat the output cap as an independent variable and report accuracy across the budget regime, not at a single budget.

</details>


### [119] [Visualizing Graph-to-Answer Mechanism Recovery in Materials-Science Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04170)
*Shashwat Sourav,Subhadeep Pal,Markus J. Buehler,Sanjay Das,Fiona Y. Wang,Dominik Soos,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个图到答案的机制追踪工作流，用于诊断AI共科学家模型Graph-PRefLexOR-8B在生成材料科学假设时是否保留了科学上有意义的机制，而非仅仅流畅的文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI共科学家能生成流畅的材料科学假设，但流畅性不代表答案保留了科学上有意义的机制。需要一种可诊断的工具来追踪模型内部机制是否真正保留科学含义。

Method: 将Qwen3-8B适配为Graph-PRefLexOR-8B，暴露头脑风暴、图构建、模式提取和综合等不同阶段；整合语义回溯、图破坏、基于激活的恢复测量和逐层逐词元区域网格，形成可视化诊断工作流；对100个开放性问题进行全扫描（嵌入输出+36个Transformer块，共37个残差流检查点）。

Result: 最终答案与模型自身结构化阶段（尤其是综合阶段）最接近；在图破坏下，早期过渡区域（层7-10）机制恢复很少，恢复主要集中在后期综合和答案起始区域（约第30层和第36层）。

Conclusion: 该工作流可帮助科学家和模型开发者识别生成的假设在何处丢失或重新获得机制支持，从而在下游实验规划之前进行干预。

Abstract: AI co-scientists can generate fluent materials-science hypotheses, but fluency does not show that an answer preserves a scientifically meaningful mechanism. We present a graph-to-answer mechanism-tracing case study for Graph-PRefLexOR-8B, a Qwen3-8B model adapted to expose distinct stages for brainstorming, graph construction, pattern extraction, and synthesis. We organize semantic backtracking, graph corruption, activation-based recovery measurements, and layer-by-token-region grids into a visual diagnostic workflow for inspecting this pathway. Across 100 open-ended materials-science questions, final answers remain closest to the model's own structured stages, especially synthesis. Under graph corruption, a full sweep over 37 residual-stream checkpoints, the embedding output and 36 transformer blocks, shows little mechanism recovery in the earlier transition region at layers 7--10, recovery instead concentrates in late synthesis and answer-start regions around layers 30 and 36. The workflow is intended to help scientists and model developers identify where a generated hypothesis loses or regains mechanism support before it is passed to downstream experimental planning.

</details>


### [120] [Test, then Route: How Language Models Execute In-Context Conditional Rules Across Models and Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04183)
*Luxshan Thavarasa,Sivasuthan Sukumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过激活修补（activation patching）技术，在多个开源语言模型中研究了上下文条件规则（如"if P(x) then A else B"）的执行机制，发现测试谓词的模块确实是模块化的，但路由部分并非抽象的路由模块，而是与具体token绑定且不可迁移的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在执行上下文条件规则时，是否像传统计算一样组装一个运行时电路——一个模块负责测试谓词（condition），另一个模块负责路由答案（routing）。这个问题对理解LLM内部机制和可解释性具有重要意义。

Method: 采用四供体（four-donor）设计下的激活修补方法，其中两个交换规则供体使条件与答案词不一致，从而揭示每层携带的是条件还是答案信息。跨三个开源模型（两个模型家族）、六种语言和五个谓词家族进行实验，设置了严格的预指定隔离标准。

Result: 中堆栈残差带携带谓词真值：修补它可使答案翻转（谓词结果翻转接近1.0，映射翻转接近0.0），18个实验单元中有17个满足严格隔离标准。路由器呈现相反特征。学习到的子空间在训练对内近乎完美翻转A和B，但迁移到新对时接近0；然而在Gemma-3-4B中跨语言迁移时达到约0.98。路由方向是token绑定且不可迁移的。

Conclusion: 测试（predicate test）是模块化的，但路由（routing）在所有探测下都不是抽象模块——它本质上是token绑定、对特定对/语言有特异性的，而非一个通用的抽象路由模块。

Abstract: When a language model follows an in-context conditional rule such as "if P(x) then A else B," does it assemble a runtime circuit with one module that tests the predicate and another that routes the answer? We probe this with activation patching under a four-donor design whose two swapped-rule donors make the condition and the answer word disagree, so each layer reveals which of the two it carries. Across three open models from two families and six languages sharing one fixed item bank, a mid-stack residual band carries the predicate's truth value: patching it reroutes the answer with predicate-outcome flip near 1.0 and mapping flip near 0.0, meeting a strict pre-specified isolation criterion in 17 of 18 cells, and the same localization holds across five predicate families. The router shows the opposite profile. A learned subspace flips A and B near-perfectly within the trained pair yet transfers to a new pair at approximately 0 in every model, while in Gemma-3-4B (the only model probed cross-lingually) it transfers at approximately 0.98 to the same pair in other languages. Under every probe we ran, the router direction is token-bound and non-transferable (largely answer-readout in Gemma, pair-specific in Qwen) rather than an abstract routing module. Test is modular; under these probes, route is not.

</details>


### [121] [Large Language Models for Low-Resource Languages: A Conceptual Framework for an Electronic Explanatory Dictionary of the Tajik Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04186)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用大型语言模型（LLMs）开发塔吉克语电子解释词典的概念框架，填补了该低资源语言缺乏全面数字词典资源的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语缺乏与高资源语言词典功能相当的综合性数字词典资源，且现代NLP技术对低资源语言的适应性有限，因此需要构建一个整合经典词典编纂方法、语言统计和LLM生成能力的词典架构。

Method: 通过对现有语言、统计和语料库资源的系统调查，提出了一个集成词法分析、词形还原、语义聚类和词典条目生成的词典架构，采用子词分词以适配塔吉克语的黏着语形态特征，并利用参数高效微调（PEFT）策略应对有限标注数据。

Result: 论文提出了首个塔吉克语解释性词典的整体概念架构，将经典词典编纂方法、语言统计和LLM生成能力统一到单一系统中，为后续开发和实际应用奠定了方法论基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为低资源语言的词典编纂提供了方法论基础，所提出的词典不仅可作为词典工具使用，还可作为机器翻译、自动摘要、情感分析等NLP应用的核心资源，适用于计算语言学和NLP系统开发者。

Abstract: This paper presents a conceptual framework for developing an electronic explanatory dictionary of the Tajik language using large language models (LLMs). The relevance of the work stems from the absence of a comprehensive digital lexicographic resource for Tajik that is comparable in functionality to dictionaries for high-resource languages, and from the limited adaptation of modern natural language processing technologies to low-resource language systems. Based on a systematic survey of existing linguistic, statistical, and corpus resources, we propose a dictionary architecture that integrates modules for morphological analysis, lemmatization, semantic clustering, and dictionary entry generation using LLMs. The choice of subword tokenization is justified by the agglutinative nature of Tajik morphology and its high morphological variability, along with a parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategy suitable for limited annotated data. The novelty of the work lies in proposing the first holistic conceptual architecture of an explanatory dictionary for Tajik that unifies classical lexicographic methods, language statistics, and generative capabilities of LLMs into a single system. The practical significance of the study is the formation of a methodological foundation for developing a full-featured electronic dictionary that can serve both as a lexicographic tool and as a core resource for machine translation, automatic summarization, sentiment analysis, and other applied NLP tasks. The paper is intended for specialists in computational linguistics, lexicography, and developers of natural language processing systems working with low-resource languages.

</details>


### [122] [Patients-like-me: A Variational LM--GNN Framework for Explainable Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04193)
*Xinyu Wang,Yixuan Li,Hanwei Wu,Qincheng Lu,Chi-Kuang Yeh,Xiao-Wen Chang,Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PLM框架，统一结合语言模型和图表征网络，用于电子健康记录（EHR）的预测任务，通过变分期望最大化算法交替优化LM和GNN，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上超越现有方法，并提供可解释的参考患者归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对EHR的文本表征缺乏患者间关系和可解释性，而图神经网络虽然能捕捉患者间关联和提供参考患者归因，但依赖高质量的患者表征，两者需要统一整合。

Method: 提出PLM（Patients-like-me）框架，统一LM-GNN结构，将局部患者语义与全局队列结构整合；引入变分期望最大化（Variational EM）算法，在监督变分目标下交替更新LM和GNN。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，PLM一致优于现有最优方法，且改进泛化到encoder-only和decoder-only两种LM骨干网络；仅需适度的额外计算开销；边掩蔽实验验证了高排名参考患者对模型预测影响最大。

Conclusion: PLM有效结合了LM的文本语义能力和GNN的图结构推理能力，在性能提升的同时提供了可解释的参考患者级解释，为EHR预测任务提供了统一的框架性解决方案。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) offer strong textual representations for electronic health records (EHRs), but they encode patient sequences in isolation and provide limited explainability. Graph neural networks (GNNs) complement LMs by incorporating inter-patient relationships and enabling reference-patient attribution, yet they rely on high-quality patient representations. We propose Patients-like-me (PLM), a unified LM--GNN framework that integrates local patient semantics with global cohort structure. To train PLM efficiently, we introduce a Variational Expectation-Maximization algorithm that alternates LM and GNN updates under a supervised variational objective. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV show that PLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with improvements generalizing across encoder-only and decoder-only LM backbones. These gains are achieved with only modest additional computational overhead. PLM also provides reference-patient explanations by retrieving influential similar patients, while edge-masking experiments confirm that the highest-ranked references have the greatest impact on model predictions.

</details>


### [123] [Hallucinations on the Board: Tool-Augmented Evaluation of LLM Chess Commentary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04240)
*S. Ashwin Hebbar,Peiyao Sheng,Sewoong Oh,Pramod Viswanath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ACT-Eval是一个评估象棋解说事实正确性和概念覆盖度的框架，将解说分解为原子声明，并通过引擎工具和专家标注的金标准来验证，发布了含325个位置-走法对的基准和五类错误分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级人类游戏引擎虽然能提供专家级评估，但缺乏自然语言解释，无法在教育上帮助专家和非专家。大语言模型虽有潜力弥补这一差距，但因领域知识有限而频繁产生幻觉，且现有评估框架无法可靠检测这些错误。

Method: 将象棋解说分解为原子声明，路由到引擎支持的诊断工具和专家标注的金标准参考，从事实正确性、概念覆盖度和走法质量判断三个维度进行评估，并发布包含325个位置-走法对的基准数据集。

Result: 事实幻觉在象棋解说中仍然普遍存在：GPT-5.4无工具时错误子声明率达22.0%，小型开源模型超过40%。工具增强显著提升了事实正确性和走法质量评估，但所有模型在专家战略和战术思想覆盖度上仍有限。

Conclusion: ACT-Eval的事实判断在人类一致性范围内，覆盖度评分与人类战略完整性评估强相关，验证了该框架作为可靠评估工具的有效性。

Abstract: Superhuman game engines in domains like chess have made expert-level evaluations easily accessible, yet they communicate what is true without the natural-language explanations that make such expertise educationally useful to experts and non-experts alike. Large language models could, in principle, bridge this gap, but they frequently hallucinate due to limited domain-specific knowledge, and standard reference-based or LLM-as-a-judge frameworks cannot reliably detect these errors. In this work, we present ACT-Eval, an evaluation framework that decomposes chess commentary into atomic claims and routes them to engine-supported tools and expert-annotated gold references to assess factual correctness, conceptual coverage, and move-quality judgment. We release a benchmark of 325 position--move pairs spanning pedagogical, tournament, and critical positions, including 125 positions with expert-verified gold atoms and a five-class error taxonomy. Evaluating leading proprietary and open-weight models, we find that factual hallucinations remain pervasive in chess commentary: GPT-5.4 without tools produces incorrect sub-claims 22.0% of the time, while smaller open-weight models exceed 40%. Although tool augmentation substantially improves factual correctness and move-quality assessment, coverage of expert strategic and tactical ideas remains limited across all models. Human calibration shows that ACT-Eval's factual judgments fall within the observed range of inter-human agreement, while its coverage scores correlate strongly with human assessments of strategic completeness.

</details>


### [124] [Towards End-to-End Multilingual Metaphor Processing: Integrating Detection, Translation, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04260)
*Jiahui Liang,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文是一个博士研究提案，旨在构建一个多语言隐喻处理端到端框架，将隐喻检测、翻译评估和联合建模三个方向整合为一个统一的计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻语言的多语言NLP处理仍具挑战性，因为成功解释和翻译需要超越字面词汇意义的推理。现有研究将隐喻检测、机器翻译和翻译评估视为独立任务，缺乏统一的计算框架将它们集成。

Method: 提出三个互补的研究方向：（1）跨语言的鲁棒隐喻检测，（2）面向隐喻的翻译评估（包括人工评估和自动质量估计），（3）连接隐喻检测与翻译评估的联合建模。研究结合语言学理论与大语言模型的最新进展，开发新的数据集、标注方法、评估基准和自动评估方法。

Result: 预期产出是一个统一的框架，能够改善多语言NLP系统在处理比喻性语言时的开发和评估能力。

Conclusion: 该研究有望填补隐喻检测、机器翻译和翻译评估之间缺乏集成的研究空白，为隐喻感知的机器翻译提供统一的开发和评估框架。

Abstract: Metaphorical language remains a major challenge for multilingual natural language processing because successful interpretation and translation require reasoning beyond literal lexical meaning. Existing research has largely investigated metaphor detection, machine translation, and translation evaluation as separate tasks, while little work has explored how these components can be integrated into a unified computational framework. This PhD proposal aims to develop an end-to-end framework for multilingual metaphor processing consisting of three complementary research directions: (1) robust metaphor detection across languages, (2) metaphor-oriented translation evaluation for both human assessment and automatic quality estimation, and (3) joint modelling that connects metaphor detection with translation evaluation. The proposed research will combine linguistic theory with recent advances in large language models to develop new datasets, annotation methodologies, evaluation benchmarks, and automatic evaluation approaches for metaphor-aware machine translation. The expected outcome is a unified framework that improves both the development and evaluation of multilingual NLP systems when processing figurative language.

</details>


### [125] [The Fairness Collapse Phenomenon: Bias Amplification in Language Models Trained on Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04268)
*Irina Proskurina,Antoine Gourru,Julien Velcin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"公平性崩溃"（fairness collapse）现象：语言模型在合成数据上进行递归训练时，社会偏见会随着代际传承逐步放大，且这种公平性退化早于标准语言建模指标（如困惑度）的明显恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明语言模型在合成数据上训练会发生模型崩溃（model collapse），但尚不清楚合成数据暴露是否会放大或减弱预训练模型中已有的社会偏见。由于语言模型已知会复现和放大人口统计刻板印象，递归训练可能形成自我强化的反馈循环。

Method: 使用Bias in Bios数据集构建受控训练框架，在多个代际中反复用合成数据训练模型，观察偏见的演化轨迹，同时对比标准语言建模指标的变化情况。

Result: 实验一致显示：公平性退化在标准语言建模指标表现出明显退化之前就已出现。偏见在合成数据污染下会"无声地"增长，而标准的崩溃指标无法及时发现这一问题。

Conclusion: 合成数据污染对语言模型训练构成关键风险：偏见可能在模型崩溃的强指标显现之前就已暗中加剧，呼吁在持续预训练中使用开放数据时需高度警惕公平性问题。

Abstract: Generative models trained on artificially generated data have been shown to exhibit model collapse, resulting in significant performance degradation. As synthetic content increasingly contaminates the training corpora of language models, this raises critical concerns about the use of open data in continued pretraining. Although previous work has demonstrated model collapse in language models, it remains unclear whether exposure to synthetic data amplifies or attenuates the social biases already present in pretrained models. Because language models are known to reproduce and amplify demographic stereotypes, recursive training on self-generated data may create a self-reinforcing feedback loop in which biased associations become progressively stronger across generations. We call this hypothesized phenomenon fairness collapse. In this work, we construct controlled training regimes in which models are repeatedly trained on synthetic data using the Bias in Bios dataset. Across experiments, we observe a consistent and concerning pattern: fairness degradation emerges before substantial degradation is reflected by standard language-modeling metrics. This result highlights a critical risk associated with synthetic data contamination in language model training: bias can increase silently before strong indicators of model collapse become apparent.

</details>


### [126] [Eliciting Intrinsic Hallucinations in LLMs via Semantically Equivalent Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04286)
*Atri Vivek Sharma,Brian Formento,Alessio Lomuscio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过对抗性优化生成语义等价但自然多样的查询变体来压力测试LLM在RAG系统中内在幻觉鲁棒性的框架，发现即使最先进的模型也会因微小语义保持扰动而显著降低上下文忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG系统通过外部知识源提升了LLM的事实准确性，但模型仍容易产生不受检索证据支持的内在幻觉。现有研究缺乏系统评估模型在此类现象下鲁棒性的方法，需要一种能够生成自然语义等价扰动来测试模型对抗内在幻觉能力的框架。

Method: 提出一个对抗性评估框架，通过对抗优化方法生成用户查询的自然语义等价变体，施加严格的语义等价约束和内在幻觉目标函数，并应用于白盒、灰盒和黑盒三种对抗攻击设置，对5个开源和5个闭源生成模型在3个数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明即使最先进的模型也高度容易受到保持语义的扰动影响，显著降低了上下文忠实度，其中GPT-5-mini的忠实度下降高达50%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了即使是最先进的LLM，其对上下文证据的忠实使用仍然脆弱，这激励了需要设计能够独立于查询表面形式进行稳健上下文扎根的架构和训练目标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often used in conjunction with external knowledge sources to improve their factual accuracy and decrease hallucinations, through methods such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). However, these systems remain susceptible to intrinsic hallucinations, where the model generates unfaithful or fabricated information that is not supported by the retrieved evidence. We propose a novel framework to assess model robustness against this phenomenon by stress-testing using natural, semantically equivalent variations of a user query found via adversarial optimization methods. We apply our framework, which enforces strict semantic equivalence constraints and an intrinsic hallucination objective, to a range of adversarial attack techniques across white-box, gray-box, and black-box adversarial settings. Evaluating these attacks on 5 open-source and 5 closed-source generator models across 3 datasets, we demonstrate that even state-of-the-art models are highly susceptible to meaning-preserving perturbations, which significantly degrade contextual faithfulness (by up to 50% for GPT-5-mini). Our findings indicate that faithful use of in-context evidence remains fragile even in state-of-the-art LLMs, motivating architectures and training objectives that enforce robust grounding independent of surface query form. Code is available at: https://github.com/atriviveksharma/intrinsic_hall

</details>


### [127] [Searching for Sound-Meaning Collisions: Graph-Based Affordance Retrieval and Multi-Evaluator Ranking for Pun Translation at CLEF 2026 JOKER Task 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04299)
*Russell Taylor,Adam Brikman,Prateek Awate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了计算化双关翻译，将Low提出的理论（寻找声音与意义的新接触点而非等价词）付诸实践，通过检索系统、语言模型生成和多角度排序架构实现双关翻译，并分析检索到的语言资源如何在翻译过程中传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双关语翻译一直是机器翻译的难题，因为双关语依赖源语言中词义与发音的独特碰撞关系，传统等价词搜索方法无法有效处理这一问题。作者希望验证Low提出的理论——双关翻译应寻找目标语言中声音与意义的新接触点。

Method: 将双关翻译建模为发现、探索和选择三个过程：(1) 检索系统在语义和语音邻域中搜索目标语言的"affordances"（声音-意义桥梁）；(2) 多个语言模型生成竞争性翻译候选；(3) 多视角生成-排序架构选择最佳翻译。

Result: 生成器能积极利用检索到的语言资源，评估器逐渐集中于更强的声音-意义桥梁，精确语音碰撞被过高比例选中。但许多双关语仍无法找到可用的affordances，表明检索是计算双关翻译的核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: 成功的双关翻译不是保留源语言词汇，而是在目标语言中发现声音与意义碰撞的新位置，这与Low提出的理论高度吻合。

Abstract: Fifteen years ago, Low proposed that pun translators should stop searching for equivalent words and instead search for new points of contact between sound and meaning. In this paper, we investigate that idea computationally. We model pun translation as a process of discovery, exploration, and selection. A retrieval system searches semantic and phonological neighborhoods for target-language affordances: sound-meaning bridges that may support new wordplay. Multiple language models then explore these opportunities by generating competing translations, while a multi-perspective generate-and-rank architecture selects among them. Beyond system development, our primary contribution is an analysis of how retrieved affordances propagate through the translation process. We find that generators actively exploit retrieved opportunities, evaluators progressively concentrate around stronger sound-meaning bridges, and exact phonological collisions are selected at disproportionately high rates when available. At the same time, many puns still yield no usable affordances, suggesting that retrieval remains the central bottleneck in computational pun translation. The resulting picture is remarkably close to the process envisioned by Low. Successful pun translation emerges not from preserving source-language words, but from discovering new places in the target language where sound and meaning collide.

</details>


### [128] [Pun Intended: Multi-Agent Translation of Wordplay with Contrastive Learning and Phonetic-Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04311)
*Russell Taylor,Benjamin Herbert,Michael Sana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用大语言模型（LLM）结合语言约束进行英语到法语的双关语翻译，提出了三种方法：基线判别器反馈法、引导推理管线法和多智能体框架法。多智能体和引导链式推理系统在CLEF JOKER 2025任务2中分别排名第一和第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双关语的跨语言翻译一直是机器翻译的难题，传统机器翻译系统难以保留源语言中的语言创意、歧义性和幽默感。作者希望通过结合LLM与语言约束来超越字面翻译，实现双关语的创造性保留。

Method: 提出了三种方法：(1)基线方法：LLM配合判别器的正负法语示例反馈；(2)引导推理管线：使用结合音韵-语义嵌入来检索候选词生成双关语；(3)多智能体框架：通过专门化反馈迭代评估和重新生成候选翻译。

Result: 多智能体框架和引导链式推理系统在CLEF JOKER 2025任务2竞赛中，在专家人工评估下分别排名第一和第二。虽然BLEU和BERTScore的提升仅为中等水平，但人工评估表现优异。

Conclusion: 显式的音韵-语义引导和迭代多智能体评估可以改善基于LLM的双关语翻译，优于直接判别器引导生成的方法，特别是在平衡语义保真度、音韵相似性和目标语言自然表达方面。

Abstract: Translating wordplay across languages has long challenged both professional translators and machine translation systems. We investigate three approaches to translating puns from English to French by combining large language models with linguistic constraints for wordplay generation. Our baseline uses a large language model with feedback from a discriminator prompted with positive and negative French examples. Our guided reasoning pipeline uses combined phonetic-semantic embeddings to retrieve lexical candidates for wordplay generation. Finally, our multi-agent framework iteratively evaluates and regenerates candidate translations using specialized feedback. Moving beyond literal translation, our objective is to preserve the linguistic creativity, ambiguity, and humor of the source-text wordplay rather than simply reproduce its vocabulary. The multi-agent and guided chain-of-thought systems ranked first and second, respectively, in the CLEF JOKER 2025 Task 2 competition under expert human evaluation, despite only modest improvements in BLEU and BERTScore. These findings suggest that both explicit phonetic-semantic guidance and iterative multi-agent evaluation can improve LLM-based wordplay translation relative to direct discriminator-guided generation, particularly when balancing semantic fidelity, phonetic similarity, and natural target-language expression

</details>


### [129] [DataRx: Missingness-Aware Sampling for Safer Large Language Model Task-Specific Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04322)
*Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DataRx，一种基于缺失感知采样的方法，用于在任务特定微调中选择安全关键样本，仅用1%的安全样本即可将Llama3-8B-Instruct的平均攻击成功率从59.23%降至13.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务特定微调会削弱对齐LLM的安全防护栏，而现有研究虽表明混合安全数据可缓解安全退化，但缺乏对哪些安全示例更有效及其背后原理的系统理解。

Method: 提出DataRx，一种缺失感知采样方法，利用高维隐藏表示而非离散token来量化目标模型原生响应与安全参考响应之间的安全信号差距，据此选择能填补LLM安全能力缺失部分的安全示例。

Result: 在BeaverTails上仅用1%额外安全样本，DataRx将Llama3-8B-Instruct在七个下游任务上的平均攻击成功率从59.23%（随机采样）降至13.70%；且可与现有安全数据合成方法结合进一步增强安全防御。

Conclusion: DataRx验证了基于缺失感知的安全数据采样能显著提升微调期间的安全保持效果，并有望激发更多以数据为中心的安全防御研究。

Abstract: Task-specific fine-tuning can improve the performance of large language models (LLMs) on downstream tasks. However, our study reveals that task-specific fine-tuning can also weaken the safety guardrails of aligned LLMs. A widely adopted strategy for preserving safety during fine-tuning is to incorporate safety data. Although previous studies have shown that randomly mixing safety data can alleviate safety degradation, the underlying principle determining why some safety examples are more effective than others still remains unclear. In this paper, we propose DataRx, a missingness-aware sampling method for selecting safety-critical examples. DataRx is based on the hypothesis that a safety sample is more effective when the selected examples provide safety signals that fill the missing parts of LLMs' safety capabilities. DataRx's key insight is leveraging high-dimensional hidden representations rather than discrete tokens to quantify the safety signal gap between the target model's native response and the safety reference response. The results show that, with only 1% additional safety samples from BeaverTails, DataRx reduces the average attack success rate of Llama3-8B-Instruct across seven downstream tasks from 59.23% under random sampling to 13.70%. In addition, DataRx can be combined with the existing safety data synthesis method to further enhance safety defenses during fine-tuning. We hope that DataRx will inspire more data-centric defense research.

</details>


### [130] [Right Reset: Chunking by Prefix Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04330)
*Mike Vegeto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Right Reset (RR)方法，通过移除因果语言模型的左侧上下文来探测"边界"，利用动态规划将边界分数转换为可变长度分块，在扁平化文本上恢复原始记录的效果显著优于传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当文本被拼接成扁平形式（删除分隔符和布局）后，传统方法难以识别原始记录的边界。作者发现移除因果语言模型的左侧上下文可以揭示一种有用的边界信号——模型在处理相同右侧token时几乎无变化的位置。

Method: 提出Right Reset (RR)探测方法，通过移除左侧上下文测量右侧隐藏状态轨迹的保持程度；使用动态规划将RR边界分数转换为可变长度分块；在由主题相似记录拼接形成的扁平文本上进行评估。

Result: RR在扁平文本上恢复了47.7%的原始记录作为干净单元，而最强传统基线BGE嵌入边界仅恢复25.9%；该优势在渲染和OCR后依然保持；被动评分和直接提示同规模指令模型的效果均明显更差；在六个语言模型上RR选择的切割点引起更少的局部输出扰动。

Conclusion: 核心贡献在于干预本身：当表面结构薄弱时，上下文依赖本身可以提供边界信号。该工作展示了因果语言模型的上下文依赖可作为无监督文本结构发现的工具。

Abstract: Removing the left context from a causal language model reveals a useful kind of boundary: an edge where the model processes the same right-hand tokens with little change. We turn this observation into prefix-removal probing and introduce Right Reset (RR), which measures preservation of the right-hand hidden-state trajectory. A dynamic program converts RR edge scores into variable-length chunks. On flattened text formed by concatenating topically similar records after deleting their separators and layout, RR recovers 47.7% of the original records as clean units, versus 25.9% for a BGE embedding boundary, the strongest tested conventional baseline without task-specific model training. The gain persists after rendering and OCR. Passive scores from the same Qwen3-4B layer and direct prompting of a same-scale instruction model perform substantially worse on flattened records. Across six language models, RR-selected cuts also undergo consistently less local output disruption than unselected candidate edges. An observed-token likelihood-ratio readout is competitive in some architectures, indicating that the central contribution is the intervention: context dependence itself can provide a boundary signal when surface structure is weak.

</details>


### [131] [Equitable System-Prompt Selection via Constrained Mixed-Strategy GroupDRO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04339)
*Mengyu Xu,Qiaoxin Yang,Zhihan Liu,Ruiyao Xu,Zachary Liu,Kezhen Chen,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于约束混合策略GroupDRO的系统提示词选择框架，通过为已有提示词池中的多个系统提示词分配权重，最小化最差情况下的信息质量损失，同时保持平均质量接近最优基线，以解决不同提问方式导致回答质量不均的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在信息检索中，语义等价但措辞不同的问题可能获得质量差异很大的回答。尽管系统提示词被广泛用于引导模型行为，但现有方法通常只优化平均情况下的质量，导致某些提问方式仍可能获得不完整或低质量的回答。

Method: 提出一个受约束的混合策略GroupDRO框架用于系统提示词选择。该框架不对提示词文本进行优化，而是对现有提示词池中的系统提示词分配权重，以最小化跨评估指标和组的最差情况信息质量损失，同时约束平均损失接近基于平均选择的损失。池生成与选择解耦，适用于任何系统提示词池，可以利用互补系统提示词的集成而非单一提示词。

Result: 在五个LLM上的两个双语医学和消费者金融基准中，约束方法相对于无缓解措施，平均降低Overall Mean 13.1%、Worst 25% Mean 13.2%、Worst 13.7%，同时整体质量接近Average选择。多提示词权重揭示了指标-组对之间的互补性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过集成互补的系统提示词并在最差情况下进行优化，有效提升了LLM回答的质量公平性，同时保持了整体平均质量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for information seeking, yet semantically equivalent questions phrased in different ways can receive answers of considerably different quality. System prompts are widely employed to steer response behavior, but they are typically optimized for average-case quality, so some question phrasings may still receive incomplete or low-quality answers. To address this, we formulate a constrained mixed-strategy GroupDRO framework for system-prompt selection. Instead of optimizing the system-prompt text, the framework assigns weights to system prompts in an existing pool to minimize the worst-case information-quality loss across evaluation metrics and groups, while constraining the mean loss to stay close to that of average-based selection. Because pool generation and selection are decoupled, the method applies to any system-prompt pool and can leverage an ensemble of complementary system prompts rather than a single one. Across five LLMs on two bilingual medical and consumer-finance benchmarks, the constrained method reduces the Overall Mean, Worst 25% Mean, and Worst by 13.1%, 13.2%, and 13.7% on average relative to no mitigation while keeping overall quality close to Average selection. Its multi-prompt weights reveal complementarity across metric-group pairs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Rainxu09/equitable-system-prompt-selection.

</details>


### [132] [The Calibration Floor: Format Repair Can Masquerade as Self-Correction at Small-to-Mid Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04355)
*Mingguang Chen,Bo Qu,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现语言模型自我修正（self-revision）的准确率变化常常被错误地归因于推理能力变化，实际上很大一部分效果来自"答案可解析性"（format/parseability）这一边界效应。通过语法定向解码进行因果验证，在14个实验单元中消除了中位数71%的差异，表明内容层面的修正只占被测量为"自我修正"效果的一小部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型自我修正后的准确率变化通常被解释为推理能力的改变，但这一解释可能忽略了答案提取边界（answer-extraction boundary）上的格式效应。现有研究多为观察性分析而非因果验证，作者希望从因果层面检验格式恢复/丢失对自我修正效果的贡献，以纠正领域对"自我修正"效果的误解。

Method: 将"总是修正"（always-revise）的准确率变化分解为内容边际（两个答案都可解析）和格式恢复/丢失边际（可解析性变化）。在多种模型（Qwen3.5 0.8B-9B、Gemma-4-12B及两个前沿API模型）的29个主实验单元+前沿模型实验组中进行分解，并通过语法定向解码（grammar-constrained decoding）强制所有答案可解析，从而进行因果测试。此外还使用聚类模型分析规模效应，并复现了参考文献中的置信度门控协议。

Result: 在12个有意义的不可解析答案率的实验单元中，格式效应显著超过内容效应（Wilcoxon p=1.7e-3）。语法定向解码在14个单元中消除了中位数71%的总效应与内容边际估计之间的差距。小规模模型（0.8B/2B）内容层面变化和伤害的几率远高于大规模模型（p<1e-7）。置信度门控协议复现失败，前沿模型实验中内容边际恰好为零（5个单元全部如此），尽管总效应高达+0.275。

Conclusion: 内容层面的修正只占领域所测量的"自我修正"效果的少数份额，大部分可归因于答案格式的可解析性变化，而非真正的推理能力改变。作者发布了完整的实验工具、代码和结果数据。

Abstract: Accuracy changes after language-model self-revision are usually interpreted as changes in reasoning. We show this can fail at the answer-extraction boundary, and test the failure causally rather than only observationally. Across Qwen3.5 (0.8B-9B), Gemma-4-12B, and two frontier models via API (Tencent Hy3, Nvidia Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B) in 29 primary cells plus a frontier arm, we decompose the always-revise accuracy shift into a content margin (both answers parseable) and format-recovery/loss margins (parseability changes). On 12 cells with meaningful unparseable-answer rates, format effects exceed content effects (Wilcoxon p=1.7e-3). To test this causally, we force already-generated reasoning through grammar-constrained decoding so every answer is parseable by construction: across 14 cells this closes a median 71% of the gap between the naive total effect and the content-margin estimate, with two cells converging exactly and a residual on the two largest-effect cells reported rather than dismissed. A clustered model confirms floor-scale (0.8B/2B) models have far higher odds of content-level change and harm than capable-scale models (p<1e-7). Replicating a cited confidence-gating protocol verbatim on Qwen3.5 does not reproduce its reported gain and shows the same near-zero content margin. A frontier check on much larger models shows format-dominance intensifying with scale: content margin is exactly zero in all 5 cells despite total effects up to +0.275, though this arm is lower-powered. The calibration-floor criterion on the content margin reveals a squeeze: floor-scale cells have headroom but insufficient signal, capable-scale cells have signal but little headroom; only one cell is marginally viable, with negligible sealed-holdout gain. Content is a minority share of what the field has measured as self-correction. We release the instrument, code, and derived results.

</details>


### [133] [FinReportBench: Measuring and Improving Institution-Grade Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04374)
*Yinghao Tang,Tan Zhenwei,Yiyao Wang,Wanli Gu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinReportBench，一个基于专家标注的金融报告生成基准，包含35项评审标准、244个中英双语任务，并通过技能蒸馏方法将专家反馈转化为可复用的生成约束，显著提升多个模型家族的报告生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽能生成流畅的金融分析，但流畅性并不等同于适合机构交付。专家评审发现报告在身份认同、机构组件、来源纪律和视觉交付等方面存在系统性缺陷，缺乏可靠的评测基准来衡量和改进机构级金融报告生成质量。

Method: 通过专家偏序、多模态证据和决策边界审计构建35项评审标准；从10,000条中英文金融研究记录中筛选出244项双语任务，涵盖三种研究对象和两种输入层级；每项任务分离公共查询、重建的研究轨迹和隐藏源数据包；使用三个独立的评审模型家族验证专家偏序的一致性；最后通过基准引导的技能蒸馏将常见失败转化为可复用的生成和自审约束。

Result: 三个评审模型家族在复现专家偏序时达到近天花板水平；九种模型家族基本可交付性已接近饱和，但报告身份和机构完整性仍是主要瓶颈；最大的跨模型差距集中在生成轨迹控制、信息密度和数据纪律。经过技能蒸馏后，五种模型家族的平均G1提升33.85分，G2提升13.83分，且G0保持不变。

Conclusion: 有界、可观察的标准能支持可靠评估，基准引导的技能蒸馏能有效将专家反馈转化为跨模型可迁移的生成改进，为机构级金融报告生成提供了可复现的评测和优化框架。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent financial analysis, but fluency alone does not establish whether a report is suitable for institutional delivery. We introduce FinReportBench, an expert-grounded benchmark for measuring and improving institution-grade financial report generation. Expert review reveals recurring gaps in report identity, institutional components, source discipline, and visual delivery. We derive a 35-item rubric through expert partial orders, multimodal evidence, and audits of decision boundaries, covering deliverability, report identity, and institutional completeness. Starting from 10,000 balanced Chinese and English financial-research source records, we curate 244 bilingual tasks across three research objects and two input tiers. Each task separates the public query, reconstructed research trajectory, and hidden source packet. Three independent judge families reproduce the expert partial order at near-ceiling rates, showing that bounded, observable criteria support reliable evaluation. Across nine model families, basic deliverability is nearly saturated, while report identity and institutional completeness remain the primary bottlenecks. The largest cross-model gaps concern generation-trace control, information density, and data discipline rather than basic report framing. We then use benchmark-guided skill distillation to turn recurrent failures into reusable generation and self-review constraints. Across five model families, the evolved skill improves mean G1 by 33.85 points and mean G2 by 13.83 points over paired no-skill runs while preserving G0 for every pair. Code and benchmark artifacts are available at https://github.com/MisterBrookT/finreportbench.

</details>


### [134] [EdgeLM: Edge Demonstrations for Language Models' Table Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04390)
*Soroush Omidvartehrani,Mohammadamin Habibollah,Mohammadreza Daviran,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EdgeLM是一个面向表格预测任务的演示检索框架，通过选择"边缘证据"（既与查询相关又包含决策边界信息的示例）来提升大语言模型的上下文学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索方法仅关注演示与查询的相似性，但相似的演示往往强化模型已有的预测倾向，而非揭示困难决策所需的区分信息，导致难以区分样本上的性能受限。

Method: EdgeLM检索两类互补的边缘证据：数据边缘（相邻但真实标签不同的样本）和模型边缘（模型先前误分类的相似示例），无需模型重训练或任务特定工程。

Result: 在五个数据整理任务、十五个数据集和五个开源及专有LLM上，EdgeLM在所有设置中均达到最佳或接近最佳性能，消融实验表明两类边缘证据提供互补收益。

Conclusion: EdgeLM通过引入决策边界信息的演示选择策略，有效提升了LLM在表格中心预测任务上的上下文学习表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform table-centric prediction through in-context learning, making demonstration selection critical to performance. Existing retrieval methods prioritize similarity to the query, but similar demonstrations often reinforce the model's likely prediction rather than reveal the distinctions needed for difficult decisions. We propose EdgeLM, a retrieval framework that instead selects edge evidence, demonstrations that are both relevant to the query and informative about the decision boundary. EdgeLM retrieves two complementary forms of edge evidence by selecting data edges, nearby examples with different ground-truth labels, and model edges, similar examples previously misclassified by the deployed model. EdgeLM requires neither model retraining nor task-specific engineering. Across five data wrangling tasks, fifteen datasets, and five open-weight and proprietary LLMs, EdgeLM consistently achieves the best or near-best performance in every setting, while ablations show that the two forms of edge evidence provide complementary benefits. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/soroushomidvar/EdgeLM.

</details>


### [135] [NOLLI: A Difficulty-Calibrated Puzzle Benchmark for Diagnosing the English-Korean Performance Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04397)
*Dasol Choi,Joonyong Park,Daegon Yu,Soo Yong Kim,Youngsook Song,Seunghyeok Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NOLLI，一个程序化生成的英韩谜题基准测试，用于诊断韩国语性能差距的根源。它包含15种谜题类型（25个任务，7500个条目），每个实例均可种子重新生成、验证唯一解并确定性评分。研究发现匹配的英韩准确率在±10个百分点内统计等价，但书写系统密集型任务（如韩文密码）表现出显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以精确定位多语言模型在特定语言（如韩语）上性能下降的具体原因，作者希望通过精细设计的谜题基准来诊断韩语性能差距究竟源于呈现语言、书写系统还是文化/规则层面。

Method: 程序化生成15种谜题类型（25个任务，7500个条目），采用三层设计：匹配的直接翻译、基于韩文jamo（音节内字母）的脚本改编、以及植根于韩国文化或正字法的韩国专属任务。通过行为校准难度，确保参照模型落在目标准确率区间内。评估15个前沿、开源权重和韩国开发的模型。

Result: 12个高于3%准确率下限的模型中，匹配的英韩准确率在±10个百分点内统计等价。韩文密码比英文落后最多达68.7个百分点，而基于相同jamo的算术谜题无系统性惩罚。韩国专属任务中，亲属关系缺陷在全部12个模型中为正向。7/15种谜题类型的规模度量未能从易到难增长。

Conclusion: 呈现语言本身对模型性能影响很小，性能差距主要源于多步骤音节内执行的难度。结构性规模不是经验难度的可靠代理指标。这些对比具有诊断性而非因果性。

Abstract: We introduce NOLLI, a procedurally generated English-Korean puzzle benchmark designed to diagnose where Korean performance gaps arise. It comprises 15 puzzle types (25 tasks; 7,500 items), with every instance seed-regenerable, verified to have a unique solution, and scored deterministically. Rather than equating harder with bigger, we calibrate difficulty behaviorally, tuning each generator until a fixed reference model lands in target accuracy bands. Its three-level design combines matched direct translations, script adaptations over Hangul jamo (sub-syllabic letters), and Korean-only tasks grounded in Korean culture or orthography. We evaluate 15 frontier, open-weight, and Korean-developed models; among the 12 above a 3% overall-accuracy floor, matched English-Korean accuracy is statistically equivalent within a +/- 10 pp margin (TOST), suggesting little cost from presentation language alone. Writing-system-intensive tasks show sharper gaps: Korean Cipher falls behind English by up to 68.7 pp, whereas Cryptarithmetic over the same jamo shows no systematic penalty, and Jamo Composition accuracy predicts Korean Cipher accuracy. These contrasts are diagnostic rather than causal, consistent with difficulty in multi-step sub-syllabic execution. Korean-only tasks separate rule-application deficits, which vary in sign, from a Kinship deficit positive in all 12. Finally, a salient size measure fails to grow from Easy to Hard in 7 of 15 types, making structural size an unreliable proxy for empirical difficulty.

</details>


### [136] [Social Pressure Breaks Majority Voting in LLM Safety Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04415)
*Yibo Hu,Jiaming Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM安全审查小组在面对共享误导性社会线索时的脆弱性：当六个模拟同行成员给出错误标签时，多数投票机制会导致面板误报率达到100%，且模型对"不安全"方向的追随远高于"安全"方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于不安全内容检测，常见的做法是组合多个模型的判断来纠正个体错误，但这种优势可能在所有模型看到相同误导性上下文时消失，这一风险尚未被系统研究。

Method: 设计了一个两轮受控实验：每个模型先单独判断项目，然后在六个模拟同行成员给出错误标签或弃权的情况下再次判断。最终判断通过多数投票组合。实验涵盖六个开放权重LLM和六个数据集，并额外使用了专有模型进行探测。

Result: 错误标签的同行消息使平均评审员误报率从静默条件下的56.5%升至87.5%，多数投票使面板误报率达到100%。模型对"不安全"推力的追随率约为75%，对"安全"推力仅约17%，存在强烈不对称性。同行弃权时面板表现优于其平均成员。

Conclusion: 共享社会线索的易感性是安全审查面板的失败模式，建议在部署前使用简单诊断测试来评估面板对这一风险的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to detect unsafe content. A common approach is to combine judgments from a panel of models to correct individual mistakes, but this benefit may disappear when every model sees the same misleading context before voting. We study this risk in a controlled two-round experiment. Each model first judges an item alone, then judges it again after six simulated peers either assert the wrong label or abstain. We combine the final judgments by majority vote. Across six open-weight LLMs and six datasets, we find that the wrong-label peer message raises the average reviewer false-alarm rate from 56.5% under silent peers to 87.5%, and majority voting raises the panel false-alarm rate to 100%. Without an asserted label, the same panel outperforms its average member. The effect is strongly asymmetric: reviewers follow pushes toward "unsafe" far more than pushes toward "safe" (about 75% versus 17%), so the panel's false-alarm rate rises sharply while its harmful-miss rate changes little. The proprietary-model probe shows substantial variation across models. These results identify susceptibility to shared social cues as a failure mode of safety panels and provide a simple pre-deployment diagnostic.

</details>


### [137] [MERaLiON-GR: Speech Gender Recognition Model for English and SEA Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04433)
*Qiongqiong Wang,Ai Ti Aw,Nancy F. Chen,Ying Lay Chiu,Yang Ding,Yingxu He,Ridong Jiang,Zhuohan Liu,Yanfeng Lu,Yi Ma,Muhammad Huzaifah,Nabilah Binte Md Johan,Nattadaporn Lertcheva,Pham Minh Duc,Sailor Hardik Bhupendra,Siti Umairah Binte Mohammad Salleh,Shuo Sun,Tarun Kumar Vangani,Jeremy H. M. Wong,Jinyang Wu,Longyin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MERaLiON-GR，一个基于大型语音编码器的性别识别系统，通过LoRA参数高效微调和ECAPA-TDNN下游网络，在英语和东南亚多语言上超越了SOTA模型Vox-Profile和Audio-LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前性别识别在大规模语音模型中表现不足，尤其是在东南亚多语言场景下，需要专用语音模型来实现准确的副语言理解。

Method: 微调MERaLiON-SpeechEncoder-2（基于conformer的大型预训练语音编码器），使用LoRA进行参数高效微调，并附加多尺度ECAPA-TDNN下游网络（含注意力池化）和轻量级线性分类器。

Result: 在英语和东南亚多语言（中文、马来语、泰米尔语、泰语、越南语、印尼语和高棉语）上的全句和片段级评估中，一致超越Vox-Profile和大型Audio-LLM。

Conclusion: 专用语音模型在实现准确副语言理解和跨语言泛化方面优于通用Audio-LLM，凸显了任务专属模型的重要性。

Abstract: We present MERaLiON-GR, a speech gender recognition system that performs binary classification (female / male) on English and Southeast Asian (SEA) languages. The model finetunes MERaLiON-SpeechEncoder-2, a large conformer based transformer pre-trained on a broad speech corpus, and applies parameter efficient fine-tuning via Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) to adapt the encoder to the gender recognition task, and appends a multi-scale ECAPA-TDNN down stream network with attention pooling and a lightweight linear classifier. Extensive evaluations across multilingual Singaporean and Southeast Asian languages (English, Chinese, Malay, Tamil, Thai, Vietnamese, Indonesian, and Khmer) show that MERaLiON-GR consistently surpasses the state-of-the-art gender recognition model Vox-Profile and a large Audio-LLM, in both full-utterance and segment level evaluation modes. The results underscore the value of dedicated speech models in achieving accurate paralinguistic understanding and strong cross-lingual generalization.

</details>


### [138] [D$^2$F-ReAG: Dynamic Decomposition and Filtering for Multi-Hop Reasoning-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04444)
*Jiaoyang Li,Junhao Ruan,Shengwei Tang,Kaiyan Chang,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出D2F-ReAG范式，通过动态判断根级推理的可靠性来控制推理深度，可靠时直接生成答案，否则将问题逻辑分解为子问题并利用验证后的推理来优化根级推理，从而提升多跳问答的效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在单跳查询中表现优异，但在需要跨文档推理的多跳问题上存在不足，现有方法（如图结构RAG或问题分解）缺乏动态分解和有效过滤，导致效率和准确性较低。

Method: 提出D2F-ReAG框架，自适应控制推理深度：先判断根级推理的可靠性，可靠则直接生成答案；不可靠则将问题逻辑分解为子问题，利用子问题得到的验证推理来优化和修正根级推理。

Result: 在三个多跳基准数据集上的实验验证了该方法在处理复杂多跳问题上的有效性。

Conclusion: D2F-ReAG通过动态分解与过滤机制，有效解决了多跳推理中的效率与准确性问题，为检索增强生成提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often generate inaccurate answers due to their reliance on static internal knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) addresses this limitation by integrating external knowledge and excelling at single-hop queries. However, it struggles with multi-hop questions that require cross-document reasoning. Existing methods, such as graph structured RAG or question decomposition, often lack dynamic decomposition and effective filtering, which leads to lower efficiency and accuracy. To overcome these limitations, we propose Dynamic Decomposition and Filtering for Multi-Hop Reasoning-Augmented Generation (D2F-ReAG), a novel paradigm that adaptively controls reasoning depth by judging the reliability of the root-level reasoning. If the root reasoning is reliable, the model directly generates the answer. Otherwise, the question is logically decomposed into sub-questions, and the verified reasoning derived from these sub-questions is used to refine the root reasoning. Experiments on three multi-hop benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in handling complex multi-hop questions.

</details>


### [139] [The Evaluator Is Part of the Experiment: Measuring Open-Ended LLM Conformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04463)
*Alicia Guerra,Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对开放式文本修订中LLM从众行为的测量协议，通过多语料库分离实验发现：全错同伴输入会导致最低质量的修订；不同评审者对相同答案的评分存在方向性偏差；锚定校准在潜在质量量表中不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有LLM从众研究主要关注在可验证标签下的离散答案翻转，但开放式修订场景中答案质量是分级、潜在且被不完美评判的，需要不同的测量策略。

Method: 提出了一个实验协议，涵盖一个主同伴条件语料库和多个分解语料库，用于分离普通重答、候选内容暴露、捆绑的同伴呈现残差以及评审者对可见同伴上下文的方向敏感性。使用四个开放权重生成器和三个基准进行验证。

Result: 全错同伴输入在所有生成器-数据集组合中产生最低质量的修订；盲评与知情评分的答案存在评审者差异（一个评审偏向同伴立场，两个偏离，一个中立，GPT-4o和GPT-5.4-mini审计也非中立）；锚定审计显示简短正确的锚点可能被误读而破坏潜在量表的稳定性。

Conclusion: 四个结论：翻转率不足以完整衡量开放式从众行为；错误同伴会损害开放式修订质量；评审者并非中立；锚定校准是必要的。

Abstract: Prior work on LLM conformity largely measures discrete answer flips under verifiable labels. Open-ended revisions require a different measurement strategy because answer quality is graded, latent, and judged imperfectly. We introduce an experimental protocol implemented across a pooled main peer-condition corpus and separately constructed decomposition corpora, allowing us to separate ordinary re-answering, candidate-content exposure, a bundled peer-presentation residual, and directional judge sensitivity to visible peer context. Across four open-weight generators and three benchmarks, all-wrong peer input produces the lowest-quality revisions in every generator-dataset cell. Blind and informed ratings of identical answers also differ by evaluator: one judge shifts toward the peer-endorsed position, two shift away, one is approximately neutral, and GPT-4o and GPT-5.4-mini audits are likewise non-neutral. Finally, an anchor audit shows that terse correct anchors can be misread often enough to destabilize the latent scale unless calibration is checked explicitly. These results support four conclusions: flip rates are insufficient as a complete measure of open-ended conformity, wrong peers harm open-ended revision, evaluators are not neutral, and anchor calibration is necessary.

</details>


### [140] [Energy- and Memory-Efficient PEFT Methods for Personalized On-Device SLMs on Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04488)
*Kuanysh Akhmetzhanov,Jurn-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种参数高效微调方法（Full FT、LoRA、LoRA+、QLoRA、BitFit）在四种小型语言模型（两类架构）上的表现，结合能量和内存指标，发现LoRA+在能量效率上最优（19/24配置胜出），QLoRA在内存节省上最优（峰值VRAM降低3.9倍），但考虑能量后最终仅在一次配置中被选中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源受限设备上的部署和个性化不可行，而小语言模型（SLM）的PEFT方法虽有前景，但缺乏跨架构、结合能量消耗的系统比较研究。

Method: 在两类架构（Transformer：TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen3-1.7B；SSM：Mamba-1.4B、Mamba-2-1.3B）的四种SLM上，比较五种微调方法，使用三种GLUE任务和三种LaMP个性化任务，以能量优先的NetScore-E和内存优先的NetScore-M两个指标进行评估，并采用严格的能量优先选择规则。

Result: LoRA+在19/24配置中获得最高NetScore-E，13/24获得最高NetScore-M，最终18/24被选中；QLoRA在Transformer模型中最多降低3.9倍峰值VRAM，但在能量约束下仅一次被选中；TinyLlama-1.1B在能量和内存指标上均表现最佳。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型配合PEFT方法为个性化设备端部署提供了实用的能量感知路径，最佳方法由主导约束决定：能量优先选LoRA+，内存优先选QLoRA。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in large language models (LLMs), deploying and personalizing them on resource-constrained devices remains impractical due to high VRAM, time, and energy costs.
